Pairing the two tables by id gives each of Ryuko's victim actions a twin, and the exporter checks each pair's gameplay records (op `0x01` motion, stops, power, handlers, rolls, display lengths) match once their action numbers, record offsets, headers, mode-0 op `0x1e` and unused set values are set aside; it refuses to export if any comparison changes. The twins that differ are recorded: his guard reactions' counter checks P+K (`0x2090`) at age 18 where Ryuko's check Hit Up, with 10-tick windows where Ryuko's weak guards use 8 (PCs 1908–1913); his crouching guard (151) adds 2 more power (PC 1929); his jump hit (78) rolls op `0x20` at PC 784 (`rand % 6 > 2` skips to 78 + 17); his KOs test and set player variable 3 at 30, KO down global 15 at 100; the launch-down stop moves next to its power, and four reactions differ in display lengths after their motion. So the port plays a Zoma victim through its Ryuko twin's rules (stops, slides, launches, landings, power, KO flow) with his own clips, timelines, rolls and held poses. His idle runs his action 1 in the standing interpreter (four displays, 55 ticks), and his HUD shows face 2001 (action 71, PC 677) and name 340 (action 64, PC 637). Authored: the fighter and opponent choices, the partners he offers (attacking by Ryuko's reach), and Ryuko's walking speed and fresh-press ground jump for him too.
- **Ryuko's moves:** her command table enters P, K (standing, crouching and air), Hit Up, DASH F/B and Jump 2 as their source actions through the generic `'script'` action, as Zoma's do (below): `export_assets.py` flattens every command entry's actions with their levels (header `r[2]`) and her default-action table's neutral states, and each move's own records give its displays, motion, boxes, responses, windows, handlers, power, sounds and objects. Her specials do too: Drill Punch and Air Drill, IORI, the axe throw (whose op `0x1f` enters EX Fire) and its axe, DST with its variants and missiles, the chuck, EX Ch and CPU IORI. So do her supers and COUNTER HIT: each super enters from its command entry and takes its own entry checks in script order, and a guard reaction's op `0x24` enters COUNTER HIT. Playing the data changed some timings from the old hand-built clips. Punch 3's second-to-last display is 30 ticks (PC 2460), not 15, so it lasts 79 ticks with its hops. Ground dashes land through Dash F lend (15), which frees their afterimage (PC 191). An air dash's fall is the dash's own (op `0x0a` to 7), so it keeps facing and pushes with action 7's boxes and the guard key waits for its landing, and one that lands before its fall has no landing handler and ends free. Kick 3's leg push box stays through all eight strikes. A crouching attack that runs out returns to neutral, which clears the stance bits on the ground (`0x411b01`), so holding down crouches through 18 again. Three rules of the generic path came with it: a handler keeps its target's record offset (punch 3's landings enter 181 + 11 and + 18), a timeline's last op `0x0a` enters its target (the air NO SUPER's fall 7 + 3, Zoma's CPU dash jump 343 into HIGH JUMP 6, which it previously dropped), and a command enters a script that moves that tick, from a window or the free state, since the motion pass (`0x40f910`) follows every script step. The specials brought more of the same. The axe (227) and DST's missiles (218) are objects with their own scripts, slots, handlers and bounds. Scripted fighters roll op `0x20` at its record (CPU IORI at age 66, PC 5887). Op `0x10` decodes its mode bit, and IORI's variable 6 decides super cancels. Their changes from the old hand-built rules: Air Drill carries a grounded victim (see Victim responses); IORI's later stages accept a super from their start once one connects; DST FAR grabs through its recovery; and a toss launches the tick it is applied. The grab contact counts toward HITS, as Zoma's grabs did. With the supers, op `0x1f`'s player variables (EX mode 0, the jump variable 1, IORI's 6, EX Fire 2's 12 and the darkening 14) share one table. The air beams now land through their handler into Dash F lend (15), where the hand-built rule forced LEND (9). A super cancel by Musashi, the Change Attack or the hidden super plays action 254's own timeline into its cancel version. A super's hit handler follows a lethal hit as every move's does.
- **Zoma's moves:** each of his moves runs its own flattened timeline as the match's generic `'script'` action (the source action in the fighter's `branch`), so his own records give its displays, motion, boxes, responses, power, sounds and objects. His command table enters an entry's action for the stance (entries 1–20: DST, Shin Spark, SPAKE, EN300, g beam, DRAGON, dragon 2 P+K/K/P, FIRE, BOMB, JUMP 2, DASH F/B, Hit Up, chuck, P and K); a cancel window passes it by that action's level, header `r[2]` (P, K, crouch and air P, Hit Up, chuck 21; air K 22; specials 31; SPAKE and DST 41; EN300 and Shin Spark 42; JUMP 2 255; DASH 0; air dash 254). Authored: his specials and supers take Ryuko's keys, U DRAGON, I FIRE, F dragon 2 P, G BOMB, E g beam, 1 SPAKE, 2 DST, 3 EN300 and 4 Shin Spark, each writing its entry's steps. Op `0x1e` (`0x413782`) stores the window at `+0xdf91`, which action changes keep and the return to neutral clears (`0x411b0c`); `0x41193a` reads its `r[1]` bits 0–2 as the mode (1 once the box has connected, `+0xdf85` above 1; 2 always) and bits 3–5 as the type (0 levels `r[2]`–`r[5]`, 1 only the action `r[3:5]`). Op `0x24` (`0x413a50`) runs `0x410a50`'s check (window `r[4]`, up to five steps from `r[5]`) and on a match jumps like op `0x0a` (`0x412968`): the P 1 → P1 HIT → P 2 → P 3 chain, the run's high jump, Hit Up's chase. Op `0x18` with a size restarts the contact counter (`0x412d2b`; `ponytail:` a box without kind bit 0 leaves it at 0, which none of his windows meets). Op `0x02` slots 2 and 3 enter the hit or guard handler at contact, slot 1 on landing (`0x411810`), and an action change clears them (`0x40e510`). His op `0x1f` checks of player variable 1 (10 after JUMP 2, 20 in an air dash) pick the air attacks' forms, and his op `0x16` checks loop the run while forward is held. Entering an action (`0x410d50`) stops a grounded player and keeps an airborne one's motion. A jump keeps the body boxes, so a branch into its own action starts with those its earlier records set. A command holds back later entries while it stays in its window even when refused, so P pressed just after JUMP 2 comes out once JUMP 2's neutral tick leaves its 10. His guard reactions run their own op `0x24` checks (Hit Up at ages 0 and 8, P+K at 18, 10 ticks each) after their op `0x10` finds a stock, into COUNTER HIT (174). The crouching responses follow the victim's input word (down, `0x40f4be`) as well as the port's crouching actions. The chuck catches with response 30 (Ryuko's 128, held), holds through op `0x07`, then tosses with response 41: Ryuko's CHUCK THROW victim action 132 (Zoma's 133, a twin but for Ryuko's op `0x20` sound roll at PC 1787 and object deletes), a launch at raw −600/−1920, gravity 90, from age 6 (PC 1793) into the knockdown (PC 1792), adding 8 power to the victim (PC 1783). Its TOMAHU hatchet (202) flies at raw 1700/−1200 and catches the toss with response 32 (Ryuko's 85, the twist) for 20.
- **Zoma's specials:** FIRE (204) first spawns nothing into slot 1 (PC 2846): a player's spawn into a slot whose object still lives (`0x412ac0`) jumps to `r[5:7]` + `r[7]` instead, here P 1 (178), or else deletes that object; its fireball (205, PC 2858) flies at raw 800 with response 50 (Ryuko's FB, 110) and a kind-4 box of 25, so a guard takes 20% of it. DRAGON (208, air 209) sets player variable 6 to 100 (for super cancels, with his supers), rises at raw −1700, gravity 80 (PC 2915), striking with response 42 and lastly 18, and lands through slot 1 into DRAGON LEND (210). Response 42 is Ryuko's 96 (Zoma's 97): stops of 13, 5 and 3 power, an op `0x20` that only skips its sound (`rand % 6 > 2` for Ryuko, PC 1159; `rand % 3 > 1` for Zoma), and a launch at raw −200/−1620, gravity 70, from age 5 (PC 1163) into the knockdown. dragon 2 (213–215) clears his body box (PC 3017), shows his empty image 2000, moves raw 15000 (P), 30000 (K) or nothing (P+K) for one tick, then jumps into its rising strike (216: 35 damage, response 18, landing into 210). g beam (219) spawns a beam (220) whose kind-7 box of 20 re-arms each display: three strikes of response 51 (FBB, 111). BOMB (222) jumps to BOMB RELORD (229, which clears it) while player variable 0 is 50, or adds 10 to it (PC 3154, op `0x1f` mode 2) and lays three marks (223) at 100, 220 and 340 source pixels; each drops a bomb (227) from 962 pixels up after 260 ticks, falling at raw 1200 with response 50 and a box of 20, which explodes (230) on a hit, a guard or the floor. An object falling to the floor (`0x41241c`, y 920) stops there and enters its slot-1 handler. Taking that handler clears only slot 1 (`0x41249a`), but taking a queued hit or guard handler (`0x412327`, through `0x410d50`) clears all six (`0x40e510`) for a fighter's objects, which every spawn marks `+0x15a` 1 (`0x412c00`–`0x412c4c`): Z-MEGA's *Fire ball 01 (202) that strikes falls on as ***ball hit (201) instead of landing into *Fire ball 02 (203) and striking again. Spawn flag bit 3 only marks the object for the blitter (`0x412cbb`, `0x40b698`).
- **Zoma's supers:** each runs from its timeline like his other moves. Ops `0x10` and `0x11` (`0x413500`, `0x413164`) are jumps (to target + `r[4]`): too little stock enters NO SUPER (233, or 232 in the air through its op `0x16`), otherwise the stock pays (`r[7]` added, clamped to 0–3); Shin Spark's op `0x11` turns it into BOMB at 147 life or more. Each super sets player variable 14 to 100, which shows his own panel (action 40, image 1009) as Ryuko's 43 does, adds its freeze to global 6 and stops the opponent with op `0x1a`. SPAKE (239, air 238) and DST (251) test variable 6, which DRAGON sets to 100: then they jump into 236 (+5 for SPAKE, +9 for DST), the super-cancel versions, which need two stocks and spawn GENGAI (235); DRAGON's window passes levels 40–41 once it has connected. SPAKE's dash carries its hit box on an object following him (242, 84 damage, response 43, Ryuko's 97). DST catches with a kind-8 box (response 30), spins its victim with op `0x07` holds, tosses it (response 31), juggles it with op `0x14` (responses 21 and 60, Ryuko's 84 and 109) and blasts it with a 120 box (response 43). Op `0x14` reaches the player that the last contact of either one's own box joined to this one (`+0xdef9`, set for both at `0x40f2da` and `0x40f2e0`; only the round's setup clears it, `0x411f7c`), held or not. The port keeps it per fighter (`caught`). A fighter's box meeting an object's body records the object, and the object's owner records the fighter: on an object, ops `0x07` and `0x14` place it as they would a player, op `0x07` also zeroing its motion (`0x412f66`) and stopping it for good (`+0x40` at −1, which only the step reads, `0x412397`), then skip the player's pose, response and held mark (`0x412f72`, `0x41310f`). So Air Drill's hit handler (209 + 12) seizes YINAN's standing dash copy (21 + 22) when a spin meets it, not YINAN. Neither op checks life. Both clear the holder's box and body slots (`0x40e550`, as an action change does), so a holder takes no hit and pushes nothing until op `0x19` sets a body again; the export drops the bodies there, and the match the boxes. Both set the pair's priorities about the holder's base, an object target's too (above). EN300 (265) rises, calls down five sun balls (270), freezes again for its cut-in (269), then drops a ball (272) whose landing bursts (273) into 6-damage strikes alternating responses 51 and 59 (Ryuko's 108, a stun), then 43 for 30. Shin Spark (279) dashes at raw 4000 with response 32, then crashes down with a 200 box. Responses 59 and 60 select a stun standing (Ryuko's 108, Zoma's 109) and in the air (109, 110; his last display lasts 20 ticks to Ryuko's 10 + 10), each adding 1 power to the victim and rolling which shaking order plays. A clip display now also carries its op `0x1e`, so each stand (20) opens its 0–50 window from its clip. SC DST (252) would jump into DST past its stock payment (251 + 4) at PC 3509 while global 10 is 10, but only KGT PCs 542 and 590 write global 10, to 0.
- **Kiki:** `export_fighter` reads `03 KIKI.PLAYER` (hash-checked) through the same layout: 20 command entries, 43 CPU entries, 490 life (147 at 30%), near distance 0, and default actions (`+0x757a`) laid out as Ryuko's up to the start (30), then win 56, lose 58, draw 64 and HUD face 71. Her victim actions pair with Ryuko's through the response table at Zoma's numbers, but for her KOs and lose action, one later (58–61). Against Ryuko's records her twins differ in: the guards' counter checks, Hit Up at ages 0, 8 and 18 in 10-tick windows (PCs 1852–1857, 1875–1880); her heavy air hit, HISA and lose action, which don't clear player variables 14, 8 and 6; her jump hit's roll, her KOs and four display lengths, as Zoma's; her uppercut, with no op `0x20`; and her HISA Down (124), which launches at raw −800 (PC 1522), not −500, as the match applies. Her moves run from their timelines as Zoma's do, at their levels: P, K, the crouching and air P, Hit Up and the chuck 21, air K 22, specials 31, ranbu and MAGIC FIRE 41, EN3 42, JUMP2 255, DASH F 30, DASH B 0 and the air dashes 254. Op `0x10` makes ranbu (247) and MAGIC FIRE (241) cost a stock and EN3 (256) three, or enter NO SUPER (235); each super sets variable 14, which shows her panel (action 42, image 1009). TATUMAKI sets variable 6 to 100 (PC 3190), and its ground landing opens a 40–41 window (PC 3206), where ranbu and MAGIC FIRE jump into SC (238, + 5 for ranbu) for two stocks, spawning GENGAI (237). Her supers reach four victim actions no earlier move did: ranbu's last strikes take response 63, 'ヒット DRAGON Fireball' (Ryuko's 125), whose records match the uppercut's (96), so it plays as the uppercut; EN3's lift takes response 82, 'ヒット DRAGON Yinan' (126), the same rise with no drift (PC 1715: raw 0/−1620, gravity 70, into knockdown); and its beam (259) alternates responses 44 and 45, 'ヒット Tatumaki <<' and '>>' (99, 100): 3 power to the victim, no op `0x1a`, a roll that skips its sound (PCs 1215, 1238), and a hop at raw ±500/−720, gravity 90, from the first tick (PCs 1217, 1240) into knockdown. K' (216) opens three follow-ups at age 29 (op `0x24`, 20 ticks): → K second hit (222), → P second shot (218, which fires shot 219) and ← K BLACK OUT (227). Second hit's hit handler enters >HIT (223), whose object 2nd OBJ V (225) holds player variable 4 at 10 for its 190-tick display (PCs 3154–3156); meanwhile second hit's op `0x1f` (PC 3083) enters 2ND rp hit (224), so the export now decodes variable 4's test. Her chuck (207) catches with response 30, holds through op `0x07`, then blasts the victim away with response 32 and 20 damage (PCs 2840, 2862–2863); its branch for global 3 above 0 (PC 2861) is the two-player version, as Ryuko's. Ranbu hit L's op `0x1f` at PC 3523 would skip its op `0x14` while global 10 is 10, which never happens. Her intro: 30 jumps to 32 for global 3 = 0 (PC 283), past its display for global 14 = 0 (PC 318), spawns Black and OP super Charge (38, PCs 320–321), charging two stocks, then jumps to Round 1 (33) + 2 (PC 323): six displays over 200 ticks, with no op `0x01` or op `0x16`, so she stands where Ryuko's walk ends. WIN calls DEMO Stand (28) once, then 'win rp' (57) three times (PCs 534, 536), 240 ticks in all; the draw calls DEMO Stand once (PC 620). Her HUD shows face 2001 (action 71, PC 666) and name 217 (action 65, PC 627). Her idle runs her action 1, rolling at PC 13 and ending at op `0x05` (PC 19). Her CPU writes every command into her history, as Zoma's does. `ponytail:` her potatoes (189, 190, from K SIT) set an op `0x02` slot 5 handler (`+0x74`), which `0x40edde` enters when the object itself is struck (POTETO HIT, 194); the port strikes only fighters, so it never fires. Authored: her keys (1 ranbu, 2 MAGIC FIRE, 3 EN3, U DRAGON O, I K', F TATUMAKI), and, as for Zoma, Ryuko's walking speed and fresh-press ground jump.
- **Chung:** `export_fighter` reads `04 CHUNG.PLAYER` (hash-checked) through the same layout: 22 command entries, 40 CPU entries, Ryuko's 510 life (153 at 30%), near distance 0, and default actions (`+0x757a`) one earlier than Ryuko's from the crouch (17–23), then start 30, win 57, lose 58, draw 64 and HUD face 73; his jumps land in 9 (PC 56). His victim actions sit three after Zoma's, his twists four (his ★変なＴＥＣＨ, 89, comes before them), and his KOs and lose action at Kiki's numbers; `CHUNG_TWINS` derives them and the response table confirms every pair. His guards' op `0x1e` names his COUNTER HIT, 177, where the others' name 174, so the twin comparison now ignores the action an op `0x1e` of type 1 names, and the export queues each fighter's guards by its twins and follows their op `0x24` into COUNTER HIT, instead of naming 149–174. Against Ryuko's records his twins differ in: Kiki's guards; his air blocks, which rise at raw −420 (PCs 2158, 2287), not −450, from their own op `0x01`; the roll of his heavy hits and the Tatumaki hops, rand % 3 > 1 to Ryuko's rand % 6 > 2, and his uppercut and its two twins, which roll as Zoma's; his jump hit, which clears variable 14 first and doesn't roll; HISA and the chuck toss, which don't roll; Kiki's HISA Down at raw −800 (PC 1582); Zoma's KOs and shock; and two displays (20, 10) after the air stun's and air rush hit's motion, where Ryuko's have three. Each plays from his own records where the match reads them (rolls, air reactions, timelines); the −800 is applied by name. His moves run from their timelines at their levels: P, K, the crouching moves, Hit Up and the chuck 21, air K 32, specials 31 (TO EX, Fire ball, Dragon, BK, orochi, jump wall move), Dragon air, air fire, lee, gesar, nin star and cpu jump wall 41, en300 42. jump move start (266) sets a wall handler (op `0x02` slot 4, PC 3846) for its rising display and clears it after (PC 3849); at a wall it enters JUMP WALL (267), which stops him and jumps to 'wall rp' (268): every 5 ticks its op `0x24` checks take K to wall kick (269, 30 damage), P or any up to wall jump jump (273) (PCs 3859–3861), until it runs out into the fall (7). cpu jump wall (410) does the same into 411. The match plays a move's wall handler as Yo's GIGA PRESS needs it; before, the jump move flew on to land. Op `0x10` makes lee (281), gesar (305) and nin star (312) cost a stock and en300 (329) three, or enter no super (275, 276); op `0x11` turns lee and gesar into Evil Lee (295) and Evil gesar (307) at 153 life or less. --TYPE-- (entry 13: ↓, neutral, ↓, Hit Up) enters TO EX (229), whose ops `0x10` and `0x15` (PCs 3182–3189) spend 50 power as Ryuko's EX Ch does, then set player variable 0 to 100 (PC 3191); while it is 100, PC 3190 enters TO NOR (230), which clears it (PC 3204). His HUD face (action 73) cycles 2002, 2001, 2000 and 2001 while variable 0 is 100 (PCs 675–692), as Ryuko's does at 10, and his ROUND 1's op `0x16` (PC 338) jumps to Change EX (35), which sets it, while down is held 150 ticks into the intro; the port's EX mode takes each fighter's value. His moves reach four victim actions no earlier move did. His crouching kick (206) and air fire (263) take response 48, 'ヒット Fire HISA' (Ryuko's 104): HISA's flight (raw −600/−1220, gravity 90, PC 1318) after 13 stop ticks and 5/3 power, with op `0x23`'s burn (mode 0, +10 red, +5 green, PC 1309) and the victim's own fire objects (403, PCs 1311–1315). His chuck's hit (222, 226) takes response 37, 'ヒット hisa fly side' (91): 13 stop ticks, 5/3 power, then flat out at raw −1500/−500 with no fall (PC 1081), with an op `0x02` slot 4 handler (PC 1079). Slot 4 (`+0x70`) is the wall: when the motion step clamps a player to the screen's edge (`0x40fb24`, `0x40fc93`), it writes the handler's action into the pending action (`+0x38`) and clears it (`0x40fcab–0x40fcb5`). Here that is 'fly side wall hit' (95), which halts the victim (PC 1129) for six 3-tick displays, then drops it at gravity 50 (PC 1141), landing into knockdown (PC 1140). En300's tosses (332) include response 57, 'Chuck Twis side' (136): the same flight with no stop, jumping into the spin loop 93, whose slot 4 enters 'Twis Side Wall' (94), bouncing back out at raw +700/−800, gravity 60 (PC 1126), into TWISING 2's spin (87) until landing. His lee's hits (283, 299) take response 61, 'Chuck GON MISS' (137): a float at raw −330/−430 with no fall (PC 1871) for its 70-tick display, then an op `0x15` that takes 40 of the victim's life, adds 5 power to it and 3 to the striker (PC 1874), and a jump into HISA (80, PC 1875), whose records run at once (13 stop ticks for both, its 5/3 power). Op `0x07` (`0x412e93`), like op `0x14`, reaches the player the last contact joined (`+0xdef9`), held or not; the port had held only a grabbed victim, but Chung's P3 hit, orochi, lee and en300 hold victims their strikes reached: en300's shock (response 33) is held at PC 5090, then lifted by response 82 and tossed through 76, 41, 56 and 57 (PCs 5144–5196). His win calls Demo stand (27) once (PC 543), spawns P3 smoke1 (188) behind a 20-tick blank display and holds image 293 (75 ticks in all); the draw calls 27 once (PC 628). His intro, as Kiki's: 30 jumps to 32 (PC 287), past its display (PC 324), spawns Black and OP super Charge (39, PCs 326–327), charging two stocks, then jumps to ROUND 1 (33) + 2 (PC 329): three displays over 150 ticks with no op `0x01`, then a call of Demo stand (27, PC 339), 200 ticks. His panel is action 43 (image 1009); his HUD shows faces 2000–2002 and name 375 (action 65, PC 635). His idle (action 1) tests variable 0 at PC 9 and, at 100, jumps to his EX stance (PC 18); the idle interpreter now evaluates op `0x1f`'s player-variable tests, seeded with the fighter's variable 0, and the scene restarts it whenever the fighter re-enters action 1 (an action change, `0x410cf0`, starts it afresh), not only once it ends. His CPU writes every command into his history, as Zoma's does. Authored: his keys (1 gesar, 2 lee, 3 EN300, U Dragon, I Fire ball, F orochi, G Burn Knock, E TO EX; jump wall move and air fire take their commands only), and, as for Zoma, Ryuko's walking speed and fresh-press ground jump.

Op `0x10` makes funnal, scc, NT and their forms cost a stock and EN3 (medeo) three, or enter no super (270); op `0x11` turns funnal, NT and EN3 into evil funnal (279), EVIL NT (295) and Evil En3 (331) at 141 life or less. Her HUD face (action 75) cycles as Ryuko's while it is 100 (PCs 786–803). New m star start sets player variable 6 to 100 (PC 3894), so a super entered from it jumps to '_-_-*-SC' (273) at its offset, as Ryuko's IORI cancels do. Comment chuck's hit (243, PC 3738) and scc's (289, PC 4489) apply response 52, 'Chuck up' (Ryuko's 134), a reaction no earlier move reached: 5 power to the victim (PC 1818), then straight up at raw −160/−2000, gravity 80, after its first display (PC 1825), landing into knockdown (PC 1824). Two calling records needed the flattener: op `0x09` enters and repeats its action at `r[4]` (`0x4129a9` stores it in `+0x7d`, `0x4126ae` repeats there), which NT's and funnal's objects use (nt fly, 314, calls Skill6 and Skill7 at + 5), and op `0x0b` (`0x412a2f`) is a one-shot call into `r[1:3]` + `r[3]` that returns after the call when that action runs out (`+0x85`, `0x412625`); Nt Rp (318) calls its own rest (+ 3, PC 4902), so it plays its three displays twice per call. The export now inlines an op `0x0b` into an action with displays as such a call; one into an action without (Ryuko's 228, Kiki's 263) stays an event the match runs at once. Her Vs. intro: 29 jumps to 31 for global 3 = 0 (PC 307), past its display for global 14 = 0 (PC 345), spawns Black and OP super Charge (41, PCs 347–348), charging two stocks, then jumps to Round BR (33, PC 350): it spawns her car (35) following her in slot 9 (op `0x04` flags 0x21, PC 364) and drives her in at raw 600 for 32 ticks after a 2-tick display (PC 367), then at 60 for 15 (PC 372), before a free copy at 35 + 10 replaces the car (PCs 375–377) and accelerates away (PC 423); its op `0x16` (PC 382) enters EX CHANGE (39) + 3, which sets variable 0 to 100 (PC 475) and jumps to the same displays in Round 1 (32 + 6, PC 476), while down is held 63 ticks in. It calls Demo stand (27) once (PC 388): 198 ticks in all. The intro now runs each display's events, as a clip's do elsewhere, so every fighter's intro plays its sounds and objects (Ryuko's sound 45, PC 349; Kiki's and Chung's objects before their displays at PCs 334 and 337). WIN calls Demo stand (27) once (PC 638), then plays four poses and loops image 197 (PC 649); the draw calls 27 once (PC 736). Her panel is action 45 (image 1009); her HUD shows faces 2000–2002 and name 427 (action 67, PC 746). Her idle has no EX test. Her CPU writes every command into her history, as Zoma's does. new m star start sets variable 7 to 10 (PC 3897), so, with variable 7's tests evaluated (added for DIDO), its LP hit sparks (383) enter HP hit (384, PC 6215). Authored: her keys (1 scc, 2 medeo, 3 NT, 4 funnal, U Dragon, I BEAM, F Coment chuck, G morning star, E -----), the start that ends her drive where the others' intros end, and, as for Zoma, Ryuko's walking speed and fresh-press ground jump.

His response table has 84 entries, not 83: the loader reads each table's own count (`0x403b87`), as the export now does, and no op `0x17` or `0x14` in any exported script names the 84th (192). His victim actions sit one after Zoma's, Twising 2 two (his ★変なＴＥＣＨ, 89, comes before it), his KOs and lose action three after Ryuko's, and each guard before its CONO and EX forms; `ANMC_TWINS` derives them and the response table confirms every pair. Against Ryuko's records his twins differ in: every guard's op `0x1f`, which past its op `0x15` jumps to the guard's EX form while variable 0 is 100 (PCs 1970, 2015, ...), and Kiki's 10-tick Hit Up counter; Zoma's jump-hit roll; Chuck Twis (131) rolling rand % 6 > 2 to skip its sound (PC 1719), which the port's toss clip doesn't draw (`ponytail:`); op `0x01` before op `0x02` in Chuck Hit (PCs 1700–1701); Kiki's HISA Down at raw −800 (PC 1631); Zoma's KO tests with Ryuko's landing wait, and Zoma's KO DOWN; Amiro's rand % 4 > 2 in the beam hits and stuns; Tatumaki >> giving him 5 power and the attacker 3 (PC 1229), not Ryuko's 3 and none, which the match keeps with Zoma's crouching guard in a per-fighter table (`OWN_REACTION_POWER`); Kiki's uppercut family with no op `0x20`; and Zoma's toss spin, rush air hit, shock and wall flights. The EX guards (152–197) repeat their guards' records with his winged costume's images, so `export_fighter` exports each guard's op `0x1f` target as a clip (`guardEx`, …) and the match draws those in EX mode; only 'time stop sit EX' (195) adds a spawn and a raw −650 slide (PCs 2566–2567), not played (`ponytail:`). His moves run from their timelines at their levels: P1, K1, the crouching moves, air P, Hit Up and the chuck 21, air K 22, specials 31 (to ex, Fire start, Jump fire, twis att new, fly move start, ball shoot start and air), supers 41 (super ball start, chuck sp start, CHUCK SP FAR) and EN 300 START 42; DASH F and B have level 0. Op `0x10` makes super ball and SUPER CHUCK cost a stock and EN300 three (PCs 3952, 4069, 4163, 4191), or enter no super (278). His EX mode is variable 0 at 100: --TYPE CHANGE-- (entry 13: ↓, neutral, ↓, Hit Up) enters to ex (242), which spends 50 power (PCs 3288–3295) and sets it (PC 3299), or enters TO NOR (243, PC 3296), which clears it (PC 3312). In EX mode op `0x1f` checks of variable 0 swap K2 NEW for K 2 (210, PC 2737), K3 NEW for K3 (216, PC 2838), k sit new for K SIT (219, PC 2920), cHUCK NEW for CHUCK (238, PC 3143) and twis att new for twis start (258, PC 3554); his HUD face (72) cycles as Ryuko's (PCs 717–735). Twis att new and twis start set variable 6 to 100 (PCs 3555, 3623), so a super entered from them jumps to '-**--*SC' (281), which costs two stocks (PCs 3936–3944) and enters super ball, SC CHuck Sp (295) or CHUCK SP FAR, as Ryuko's IORI cancels do. EN300's cut-in spawns CUT IN MOVIE (305, z 50) over its background (304, z 49) as viewport objects (PCs 4209–4210); the scene drew every viewport object behind the fighters at one depth, so the background hid the movie, and now orders each depth band by z. His idle (action 1) rolls rand % 3 > 1 at PC 14 into an op `0x09` (PC 20) that calls its own PCs 24–27 three times, then returns to PC 21: the idle interpreter now runs op `0x09` calls as `flatten` does (`0x4129a9` enters at `r[4]`; running out repeats there or returns, `0x4125ea`, `0x4126ae`), which the handoff's Python oracle doesn't cover, so a test pins the sequence. His Vs. intro: 32 jumps to 開始時 BR (34) for global 3 = 0 (PC 310), past its display for global 14 = 0 (PC 348), spawns Black and OP SUPER CHARGE (41, PCs 350–351), charging two stocks, then jumps to Round 1 (35) + 2 (PC 353): 200 ticks, no op `0x01`, so he stands where Ryuko's walk ends; its op `0x16` (PC 363, 156 ticks in) enters 'Chage Character' (39), which sets variable 0 to 100 (PC 405) and jumps back to Round 1 + 10 (PC 406), while down is held. WIN calls DEMO STAND (30) once (PC 571), then shows four poses and holds image 324 (PC 582); its op `0x20` at PC 572 would enter Win 2 (60) four times in eleven, a jetpack flight up at raw vy −300 (PC 586), which the clip doesn't play (`ponytail:`), and PC 577's roll picks sound 4 or 6: clips now follow an op `0x0a` forward within their action (PC 579), so his plays the fall-through's 6 alone, and no other clip changed. The draw calls 30 once (PC 675). His panel is action 45 (image 1009); his HUD shows faces 2000–2002 and name 183 (action 69, PC 682). His CPU writes every command into his history, as Zoma's does; his CPU-only entries are cpu dash (374: raw 680, then 200) and cpu DJ (375), the dash's start jumping into High JUMP (6), with buttons `0x40` and `0x200`. Authored: his keys (1 SUPER CHUCK, 2 super ball, 3 en300, U fly move, I fire ball, F twis att, G ball fire, E --TYPE CHANGE--) and, as for Zoma, Ryuko's walking speed and fresh-press ground jump.
- **Practice partner:** `dummy.ts` turns each behavior into input for the partner, so it plays through the same rules as the player. Its behaviors, the one-second attack interval, the 250-pixel jump-in distance, and both helpers are authored for training (Low health holds the player one point under the 30% threshold, since Ryuko's hidden super, Zoma's Shin Spark and G Suneo's hh take their desperation forms only below it, op `0x11` at 153, 147 and 150 or more); its punch and air kick wait until the opponent is within the move's recovered reach; they are not the original CPU. The Original CPU choice runs the recovered CPU instead (see CPU below), guard roll included.

Press Hit runs on at raw 1100 (PC 3290) into a 0-tick display (PC 3297) until its own wall handler (op `0x02` slot 4, PC 3291) enters Press Hit + 15 at the wall: the match keeps a move's slot 4 handler (+0x70) and queues it at a wall as it does a flight's (0x40fcab), and the export follows slot 4 targets as it does slot 1's. There op `0x07` holds the victim 116 source pixels ahead (PCs 3302, 3305); the wall stops them, and its transfer (0x40fb08) pushes her back that far. It then applies 43, FLY NO HIT, and takes 99 (PCs 3309–3310): 100 in all. Its op `0x1f` checks of global 10 = 10 (PC 3284) and global 3 > 0 (PC 3308) keep their fall-through. Her moves run from their timelines at their levels: P1, K1, P sit, K sit, P J, Hit up and the chuck 21, K J 22, specials 31 (Run new, Dragon start, air fire start, Zombi chuck, tofu att, CPU RUN), supers 41 (rugal, snake super 2, hidden start), en300 start 42; the dashes 0, the air dashes 254 and the double jumps 255. Op `0x10` makes rugal, snake and HIDDEN cost a stock (PCs 3244, 3189, 3363) and en300 three (PC 3566), or enter no super (230). hidden start enters tofu att (225) at 144 life or more (op `0x11`, PC 3361). Zombi hit (220) bites for 35 and heals her 6 (op `0x15`, PC 3011) after seven passes of ZOMBI RP (223) heal 2 each (PC 3056): 0x40e7c0 adds a gain up to full life (0x40ea9f), which the match now applies; no script lowers its own. It sets variable 6 to 100 (PC 3014) and, with two stocks or more (op `0x10`, PC 3016), opens a window for levels 40–41 (PC 3017), so rugal, snake or HIDDEN entered there jumps to `*-*-*-SC super` (233, PCs 3242, 3187, 3360), which takes two stocks (PCs 3172, 3176, 3181), spawns GENGAI (232), a cut-in, and rejoins the super (HIDDEN's only below 150 life, PC 3180). She has no EX mode: no --TYPE CHANGE-- entry and no intro check. Her HUD face (73) shows 2001 (PC 728). Her Vs. intro: 開始時 (32) jumps to 開始時 BR (34) for global 3 = 0 (PC 331), past its display for global 14 = 0 (PC 367), spawns Black and OP super Charge (40, PCs 369–370), charging two stocks, then jumps to Round 1 (35) + 5 (PC 372), which rides in on her robot at raw 110 from PC 378 to PC 385, spawns RObort (31, PC 388), which drives off at raw 100 (PC 315) as it fades (op `0x23`), and calls Demo Stand (29) three times (PC 392): 220 ticks. 勝ち (58) calls Demo Stand once (PC 596) and holds image 350 (PC 602); the draw calls it once (PC 681). Her panel is action 44 (image 1009); her HUD shows name 390 (action 66, PC 688). Her CPU writes every command into her history, as Zoma's does; cpu dash, cpu run att and CPU DJ (entries 20–22) enter cpu dash (318), CPU RUN (320) and cpu DJ (319). Authored: her keys (1 GIGA PRESS, 2 HIDDEN, 3 snake, 4 en300, U Dragon, I TOFU, F ZOMBI WALK, G Run in; Fire ball, only in the air, has none), and, as for Zoma, Ryuko's walking speed and fresh-press ground jump.

Their windows take levels 30–50 on contact, or in EX 25–50 (PCs 3136–3138, 3420–3422), so EX normals cancel into Dash F too; their hits set variable 6 to 10 (PCs 3165, 3229, 3455, 3517, 3776, 3826), which Dash EX tests for Dash EX CC (16, PC 305). P sit Nor (228) strikes 10, 10 and 5, K sit Nor (230) 20, P sit (229) 25 and K sit (231) 20. P J and K J then test variable 1 (PCs 3856–3857, 3998–3999, 3940–3941, 4138–4139): 10, which Jump 2, Jump 2 F and jump 2 B set (PCs 141, 171, 205), enters their 2 forms, and 20, which Dash A F and B set (PCs 380, 434), their D forms. Hit up (247) and Hit up Nor (249) rush at raw 1000 (PCs 4239, 4270) into a 15 and their hits (248, 250), whose ↑ checks (PCs 4260, 4295–4301) enter Jump cancel (251) within 20 ticks. The Chuck (252) grabs (box kind 170) into Chuck hit (253): 30 (op 0x15, PC 4364), or for Normal Chuck hit Nor (255), 35. Dash F (13) runs at raw 700 (PC 247), its op 0x16 (PC 273) looping it from PC 244 while → is held; its checks (PCs 251–272) take ↑ into Dash F Jump (14) within 5 ticks and → K into Savage (389) within 10. Dash B (17) hops back; Dash EX (15) and Dash B EX (18) dash in EX (PCs 239, 343), Dash EX taking → K into Move (391, PC 316). **Normal specials:** Driver (entry 10, ↓→ J, 268) strikes 10 and sets variable 6 to 100 (PC 4551); its checks (PCs 4579–4605) and Driver Hit's (269, PCs 4615–4631) take ← J into Boxer (276), ← K into Venom (282), → J into ARX-7 (270) and → K into Codarl (273), each with follow-ups of its own (ARX > BOXER, Codarl Jump Cancel, Boxer 2). Its window takes levels 40–41 on contact (PC 4552). Assault (entry 9, ↓← J, 337) rushes at raw 1600 (PC 6119) into Assault Hit (338), 40. DOGE Start (entry 8, ↓← K, 394) dodges back at raw −700 (PC 7163) into Doge RP (395), whose checks (PCs 7179–7182) take J into DOGE P (402), K into DogeMidKick (400), Chuck into Doge Chuck (396) and H into Dragon (291) within 5 ticks; in the air it is DG A (294). ECS P and ECS K (entries 11–12, ↓ ↓ J or K, 320–321) speed up at raw 200 per tick (PC 5661), unseen (PC 5666) and tinted after (PC 5669), through the opponent. **EX specials:** PSt J (286) rushes into PAtt (287), Fist S (345) fires Fist S SF (346) volleys, BTK S (314) counters (above), Flash P and K (325–326) throw a flashbang, and DG A's EX form p90 (299, PC 5246) fires P90 bursts. Fist S and p90 count their passes in their object's own variable 0: op `0x1f` below selector 0x40 reads and writes `esi + 0x131` (`0x4137d9`), not the player's, so Fist S sets it (PC 6304), adds 1 each volley (PC 6327), takes two volleys while it is 1 or less (PC 6315), and more while P is pressed until it passes 5 (PCs 6317–6318). The export now decodes these (`local`, and a check's `pre` set or add, `0x4138df–0x41391d`), and each fighter and object keeps its own (`locals`, zeroed as an object spawns). `ponytail:` with `r[5]` bit 0x80 a record takes its operand from `r[6]`, a position among them (`0x413813–0x4138cf`), so Flash Att's checks (PCs 5785–5786, ...) keep their fall-through. **Normal supers:** SS (entry 3, ↓→↓→ J, 351) spends a stock (PC 6416), freezes 10 + 40 ticks (PCs 6420–6427) and aims, looping PCs 6440–6444: P (PC 6441) fires SS 1, 2 and 3 (353–358), then SS fin (359), and K (PC 6442) enters SS K (360). Each shot spawns a screen-wide box (SS 1st–3rd, 362–364: 10, 15, 20; SS Fin 75, or for rand % 6 > 4 SS Fin S's 115, PC 6651) whose response 82 lifts the opponent in DRAGON Yinan, above the next shot. Its op `0x1f` checks of global 15 (PC 6434, ...) keep their fall-through, so the aim holds until a button. Driver Shield (entry 4, ↓→↓← K, 411) spends a stock (PC 7451) and raises Driver Shield df, whose box (10, kind 4, PC 7486) enters Driver Shield Hit (417, slot 2), the shield's 70 (419, PC 7659); its DS Fire df (422) sets variable 2 to 100 on a contact (423, PC 7699), which Driver Shield df tests (PCs 7487–7507) for tobi Hit too. Venom Doll near and far (entry 1, →↓←→↓← J, 452–453) spend a stock (PC 8298) and grab along the floor (box kind 42, PC 8326) into Venom Doll Hit (456, slot 2): 50 (PC 8481). EN3 Start (entry 6, ↓→↓→ K, 481) needs three stocks (PC 8918). R100 (entry 5, ←→←→ J, 519) and R300 (entry 2, →←↓→ K, 532) jump to Driver at 150 life or more (op 0x11, PCs 9971, 10272). R100 spends a stock (PC 9973) and leaps at raw 750, −1400 (PC 10003) with a box that skips grounded victims (kind 0x10, PC 10011) into R100 Hit (523), whose landing (524) heals her 4 and 14 life (op 0x15, PCs 10112, 10126), 50 in all, and strikes 80 (PC 10203). R300 needs three stocks and spends two (PC 10276), fires R3 att (535, slot 9, PC 10300), whose contact drops it (536) into R3 att Guard Land att (541), which takes 100 more power (PC 10531) and bursts (box kind 70, PCs 10536, 10542); the burst's hit (561) sets variable 1 to 2 (PC 11027), which R300 tests (PCs 10310–10350) for R300 RP (533), which jumps at once to R300 Hit (534, PC 10355), an unseen dash at raw 3000 (PC 10427) whose box (PC 10430) answers every stance with response 80, the air time stop guard, and enters R300 S (542, slot 2, PC 10428): her friends WW, Yonan, PW and Rinzi (548–551) and R300 Yinan (552) into the finale, 228 (PC 10931). The engine decides a contact's guard from the guard half (`0x40f4be`), cleared for an unguardable response (`0x40f544`), never from the reaction it names, so the match now counts a contact as guarded only when the victim guards and its reaction is a guard: R300 Hit's time stop is a hit. After Driver's hit (variable 6 = 100), SS, Driver Shield, Venom Doll and R100 enter their SC forms (352, 414, 454, 520, PCs 6412, 7449, 8296, 9970), which need two stocks and spend them (PCs 6450, 7522, 8383, 10021) and spawn GENGAI (408). **EX supers:** Trap St (370), FB St (426), Call Lin (463) and MUSIKI St (508), the last needing three stocks (PC 9667). FB Loop calls FB M into its freeze wait (PC 8074), whose check skips past the called action's end; the export now drops such a check, which returns from the call either way (`0x4125ea`). Results: 勝ち (79) calls Demo Stand (36) once (PC 908) and loops her last two poses from PC 922 (PC 924); the draw (98) calls 36 once (PC 1162) and loops from PC 1177 (PC 1193). Each first enters DEMO air (37) while airborne (op 0x16, PCs 905, 1161). `ponytail:` 勝ち's rand % 6 > 3 rolls into Win 2 (82) and Win 3 (90) (PCs 906–907) aren't played, nor her EX results, for variable 0 = 100: Demo Stand's EX stance (35, PC 543), Win 1 EX (81, PC 909) and the draw's Stop (97, PC 1163). - **DoraEmon:** `export_fighter` reads `46 B6.PLAYER` (hash-checked), whose name field reads `46 DoraEmon`: 43 command entries, 57 CPU entries, 600 life (180 at 30%), five stocks of 90 power (see Power gauge) and near distance 150, which splits コマンド 1's HP and HK starts; his jumps land in Lend (10, PC 47). The slots before it, `43 B3`, `44 B4` and `45 B5` (named b3, b1 and b2), are blank templates: 24 empty actions, one to nine placeholder images and the shared sound bank, so the port has nothing to play from them. His file is SEAN's reworked, keeping SEAN's name image (247, *name 83, PC 1047) and sprites (634–645). His twins sit 25 after Ryuko's hits to her J atttack (he lacks her J atttack 2, 78), 24 from her DOWN to SHOCK INFINITY, 23 from her Shock rp (his ★変なＴＥＣＨ, 89, sits among his basic actions), 22 past her Chuck Hiso (139), which he lacks, and 23 again from her guards, past his *gard eff (171); his lose action 負け (68) jumps to KO NORMAL (69, PC 771), which tests variable 3 for 30 into >KO NORMAL 2nd (71, PC 777) and lands into KO down (76, PC 796). `DORA_TWINS` derives them, and the response table confirms every pair. `differ` records his reactions as SEAN's but where noted: his guards clear variable 14 first and their counter checks take B without a direction (`0x2021`) where SEAN's take it with any; his air time guard holds one 26-tick display; his heavy and J attack hits roll rand % 3 > 1 before their power (PC 1145, ...); HISA Down launches at Ryuko's raw −500 (PC 2045) and gives the striker no power (PC 2037, `OWN_REACTION_POWER`); Down Get up clears 13 and holds 20 ticks first, 40 in all; and his lose action tests variable 0 for 10 into Start KO 3 (44, PC 763), which only his Start KO intro sets (PC 544), and 13 into make hikari (77, PC 769). KO NORMAL also tests global 3 for 10 into KO NORMAL CPU (70, PC 776), which Vs. play leaves at 0, and with three stocks (op `0x10`, PC 775) enters KO RELOAD (72), which spends 270 power, darkens and refills his life through Stop (79) after the round is decided; `ponytail:` KO RELOAD isn't played, so his KO is KO NORMAL's. His darkening is variable 14: each super sets it (PC 4401, ...) and his Black (50) tests it at 100 (PCs 582, 587) and clears it (PC 588). **Buttons:** the source's six buttons A–F are bits `0x10`–`0x200` (`0x414340`; GAME.INI gives player 1 the keys A, S, D, Q, W and E). Every other fighter's commands use A (K), B (Hit Up), D (P) and E (chuck) and leave C and F to CPU entries; DoraEmon's attacks take two each: LK A and B (`0x30`), HK B and C (`0x60`), LP C and D (`0xc0`), HP D and E (`0x180`), his chuck C, D and E (`0x1c0`) and EN E and F, held (`0xa300`). A button step needs none of its buttons held the tick before (`stepMatches`), so both keys would have to go down on one tick; authored, each of his keys presses both (`OWN_BUTTONS` in `commands.ts`). His specials' and supers' commands allow 10 ticks (Ryuko's 40–90), his dashes 3 and Jump 2 one: Jump 2's two steps can't both match, since the scan runs one pass per window tick (`0x410871–0x41087c`), so he has no double jump and his J attacks' 2 forms (211, 214, 217, 220) never play. **Normals:** LP (201) strikes 5 and takes levels 22–50 on contact (PC 2740), so LK, HP, HK, the specials and the supers cancel it, but not LP; LK (202) strikes 8, HP (203) 12 and HK (204) 12; crouching, S LP to S HK (205–208) strike 7, 10, 15 and 12. J LP, J LK, J HP and J HK (209, 212, 215, 218) test variable 1 (PCs 2905–2906, ...): 20, which the air dashes set (PCs 224, 245), enters their D forms (210, 213, 216, 219). → with LP enters SP1 6LP (223), a dashing punch, 25, and → with LK SP2 6LK (225), a dashing kick, 30. Hit up (231, ↘ with HP, `0x2183`) strikes 15 into hit up hit (232), whose ↑ check (PC 3322) enters Jump cancel (233) within 20 ticks. Chuck (234) grabs (box kind 168) into Chuck hit (235), 30 (PC 3385). EN up sets variable 2 to 2 (PC 3407), spawns EN up eff (246) in slot 9, whose loop (PC 3535) runs until a neutral action's op `0x04` empties the slot (通常立ち's PC 11, which the standing interpreter now passes over to the match), opens a level-2 window (PC 3409) that the held entry keeps re-entering, and adds 4 power a tick (PCs 3412–3424, 3432–3444). At five stocks its op `0x10` checks (PCs 3404, 3428, ...) enter 挑発 (241), a taunt that gives the opponent 20 power (PC 3451), or EN up 限界 (245). **Specials:** 機猫神空砲 HP (entry 17, ↓→ HP, 260) spawns 空砲 HP (262) 132 source pixels ahead (PC 3791), flying at raw 1200 to strike 40, so it passes over an opponent close in; its LP form (259) blasts 45 up close. 機猫神天翔 HP (entry 13, →↓↘ HP, 278; in the air 280) rises striking 20, 15, 15 and 10 into 突進 Hit Land (270). 機猫神突進 HK (entry 19, ↓→ HK, 267) rushes for 50. 機猫神双槌 (entries 15–16, ↓← P, 250 and 252), a dashing punch into an uppercut, and 機猫神瞬移 (entries 21–24, ↓↓, 283–286), a teleport through the Anywhere Door (287) at raw ±24000, have no key. **Supers:** 魔神空大砲 (entries 10–11, ↓→↓→ P, 296) spends a stock (PC 4400), or enters No super (290), which adds 10 power five times (PCs 4353–4356), and spawns 大砲 EFF (300, PC 4424), a shockwave at raw 1500 that strikes 130. 秘神具騒嵐 (entries 6–7, ↓←↓→ P, 305) spends a stock (PC 4605) and calls 神具 SF (307) 15 times (PC 4631), which spawns Att1–7 (308–314), green shells and gadgets that re-enter themselves on each hit or guard (slots 2–3), then 神具 Fin (315) twice (PC 4632), spawning FA1–7 (316–322): up to 457. 鬼神双乱槌 (entries 8–9, ↓←↓← P, 327) spends a stock (PC 5522) and calls 乱槌 SF (329) 28 times (PC 5544) into 乱槌 Fin (330) and 鬼神双乱槌 Fin (332): 258. 覇神極突貫 (entries 4–5, ↓→↓← K, 338) spends a stock (PC 5753) and rushes into 突貫 Hit (340) and 突貫 Fin (341–342): 215. 激神超抱擁 (entry 1, D, D, →, A, then E, 347) needs three stocks and spends them (PC 5943), grabbing (box kind 234) into 激神超抱擁 Hit (348), which opens the Anywhere Door and takes 270 (PC 6027, 349). - **RARIO:** `export_fighter` reads `47 RARIO.PLAYER` (hash-checked): 16 command entries, 27 CPU entries, 450 life (135 at 30%), three stocks of 100, near distance 0, and default actions (`+0x757a`) with his crouch at 12–18, start 24, 勝ち 50, lose 51, draw 57, HUD face 63 and shadow 64; his jumps land in lend (9, PC 46). His twins sit eight before Ryuko's KOs and lose action, seven before her other reactions up to SHOCK INFINITY, eight from her Shock rp (his ★変なＴＥＣＨ, 89, follows his DRAGON P), seven again from FLY NO HIT, three from her Chuck GON MISS (his DOWN section, 130–133, comes before it), four past her Chuck Hiso (139), which he lacks, and fourteen before her DOWN section; his guards follow one after another from 141. `RARIO_TWINS` derives them, and the response table confirms every pair. `differ` records his reactions as ASTROMAN's but where noted: HIT UP as Z-MEGA's (its variable 8 test carries 100 in `r[7:9]`, which a test doesn't read); the knockdowns clearing variable 5 (PCs 1513, 1529) where ASTROMAN's clear 1; Down Get up on 12-, 8- and 10-tick displays; HISO stop holding 36 ticks (PC 1190), as TTRO's; MUSIC ATTACK as Z-MEGA's without his roll; Chuck Yo as DoraEmon's; and his KOs. His HISA Down flies at −800 (PC 1337, `LAUNCH_VX`). **KO:** 負け (51) clears variables 14 and 8, tests global 0 for 10 into make hikari (55, PC 464) and jumps to KO NORMAL (52, PC 465), which tests variable 3 for 30 into >KO NORMAL 2nd (53, PC 469). Both launch straight up at raw 0/−1820, gravity 90 (PCs 487, 500), where every other fighter's fly back at −500/−1520, and land into KO down (54), which has no slide (PC 508): image 207 shows lower every 2 ticks, 11 to 226 source pixels down (PCs 509–516), then image 8191 for 50 (PC 517), looping there while his life is 0 (PCs 518–520). A grounded KO rises 446 arena pixels (Ryuko's 309) and lands 67 ticks after the launch. **Buttons:** his commands use only P (D) and K (A); his Jump 2, Hit Up, Chuck and コマンド 1 entries name no action, so he has no double jump, Hit Up or chuck. **Normals:** P (158) strikes 18 and takes levels 22–50 on contact (PC 1753), so K, the specials and the supers cancel it; K (159) steps in at raw 500 (PC 1775) and strikes 15, and its second box (kind `0x8b`, PC 1783) skips a reeling victim, so it lands once; K's window takes only 30–50 (PC 1770). P J and K J (160, 161) strike 15 and run out into 空中落下 (7, PCs 1808, 1826). Dash F (10) runs at raw 900 and Dash B (11) at −900. **Specials:** Fire ball (entry 6, ↓→ P, 163) enters P instead while its slot 0 object lives (op `0x04`'s busy check, PC 1829), and otherwise spawns fire (164, PC 1842) 127 source pixels ahead, flying at raw 600 under gravity 80 to strike 30 (response 50, FB) and landing into fire bunce (165, slot 1), which hops on at −1100 until it strikes or is guarded (slots 2–3, into Nothing, 268), which clears its landing handler (`0x40e510`), so it strikes once. Shoryu start (entry 4, →↓↘ P, 168) rises at −1400 striking 35 (response 17, HISA), spawns block 01 (170, PC 1979) at its peak, a ? block, and lands in Shoryu lend (169). The block spawns RANDON OBJ (171, PC 1997), which rolls rand % 5 > 1 into randon 01 coin (172, PC 2020), then rand % 4 > 1 for randon 02 mach big (173), randon 03 Mach pois (177) and randon 04 1UP (181) in turn (PCs 2021–2023), and otherwise falls through to the coin (PC 2024). The coin adds 2 life and 10 power to RARIO (an object's op `0x15`, PC 2027), past its test of global 10 (PC 2026), which Vs. play leaves at 0. The mushrooms bounce along the floor with unguardable 1-damage boxes (kind `0x40`) naming responses 65, 66 and 67: every fighter's 'ヒット RARIO grow' (Ryuko's 115), which adds 31 life (RARIO's PC 1215), 'ヒット RARIO YARA' (116), which takes 29 (PC 1232), and 'ヒット RARIO 1UP' (117), which adds 50 life and 100 power (PC 1247). They can only meet the opponent: round setup gives each player a hit mask (`+0xdfb7`, `0x411cc0`) of every player but itself, which the hit test checks against the striker's player (`0x40f118`). The victim plays its own copy from its timeline (`OWN_TIMELINES`): grow and YARA flicker for 60 ticks (op `0x23` mode 1, PCs 1502, 1523), and 1UP, with no display, runs its op `0x15` and runs out at once; each mushroom's box takes 1 besides. Tatumaki start (entry 5, ↓← K, 186) hops at 520/−800 (PC 2288), calls tatu twis (187) three times (PC 2292), whose boxes strike 18 ahead and behind, and lands at 186 + 17 (slot 1, PC 2293): two hits, 36. **Supers:** shinkuhado start (entry 2, ↓→↓→ P, 193) spends a stock (op `0x10`, PC 2337), or enters No super (190), which adds 10 power (PC 2328); it freezes the opponent 60 ticks, leaps at 500/−900 (PC 2358) and spawns super ball (195, PC 2361), a red shell rolling at raw 1000 that strikes 84 (response 58, FBH). Shinshoryu start (entry 1, ↓→↓→ K, 200) spends two stocks, entering No super at one (PC 2474), spawns SUPER EN300 (256) and **SUPER STAR (199), and its grab box (response 30, PC 2516) enters Shin Shoryu hit (202, slot 2), which holds the opponent (op `0x07`) through a leap (PC 2576) and jumps through Shin shoryu rp to rp7 (204–210). Each stomps on the opponent's head (op `0x14`, response 5, PC 2629, ...), takes 5 or 4 of his power and 2 of theirs and spawns a *SCROLL coin (203) worth 2 life to him; then it calls Shin Shoryu RP 1up (212) five times (PC 2582), each with a *1up (211) worth 3, and jumps to Shin shoryu rp final (215, PC 2583), which takes 50, 50 and 100 of their life (PCs 2768–2773), flings them (response 43, FLY NO HIT), plants *STICK (213), a flagpole flying his R flag, and lands in Final lend (218): 200 in all, about 10 seconds. Its tests of global 10 (PC 2548) and global 3 (PC 2761, into rp final ZA, 216) keep their fall-through. His darkening is variable 14: each super sets it (PCs 2338, 2475) and his Black (36) tests it at 100 (PCs 315, 320) and clears it (PC 321). **Results and intro:** 勝ち (50) calls Demo stand (22) once (PC 452), plays sound 21 (PC 454) and loops three poses from PC 456 (PC 460); the draw (57) calls 22 once (PC 537) and loops image 227 from PC 541 (PC 542); each first enters DEMO air (23) while airborne (op `0x16`, PCs 451, 536). 開始時 (24) jumps to 開始時 BR (26) for global 3 = 0 (PC 193), which for global 14 = 0 jumps past its display (PC 229), spawns Black and OP super Charge (32, PCs 231–232), which charges two stocks, and enters Round 1 (27) + 3 (PC 234): the green pipe he rises from, 44 ticks still, a hop at raw −1000 under gravity 30 (PC 246), 50 ticks hanging (PC 248), and a hop under gravity 80 (PC 251) that passes the floor on its 56th move (`RARIO_LANDING`, 175) into Round 1 + 19 (slot 1, PC 250): 200 ticks in all. Its flattening runs on into Round 1 + 19's poses, which the port cuts before the landing. HUD face 2001 (63, PC 584); *name (58) shows 235 (PC 546); darkening image 1011 (36). Authored keys: Shinku and Shin Shoryu on 1–2; HADO, DRAGON and tatumaki on U, I and F.
- **TRAINING:** `export_fighter` reads `48 TRAINING.PLAYER` (hash-checked): RARIO's practice copy, drawn with his sprites, with 10 command entries, 27 CPU entries, 500 life (150 at 30%), three stocks of 100, near distance 0, and default actions (`+0x757a`) with his crouch at 12–18, start 24, 勝ち 34, lose 35, draw 40, HUD face 44 and shadow 45; his jumps land in lend (9, PC 54). His twins sit 24 before Ryuko's KOs and lose action, 26 before her other reactions up to SHOCK INFINITY, 27 from her Shock rp to ERUS INATUMA, 26 from her INATUMA rp (his ★変なＴＥＣＨ, 92, follows his ERUS INATUMA), 22 from her Chuck GON MISS (his DOWN section, 111–114, comes before it), 23 past her Chuck Hiso (139), which he lacks, and 33 before her DOWN section; his guards follow one after another from 122. `TRAINING_TWINS` derives them, and the response table confirms every pair. `differ` records his reactions as RARIO's, none clearing variable 14, which nothing of his sets or reads, but where noted: the knockdowns as ASURA's, clearing variable 1 (PCs 1400, 1415); >KO NORMAL 2nd holding its last pose 1205 ticks (PC 416); KO down without its global 15 test; his lose action as OS FENG's; Chuck 消える clearing nothing; ERUS INATUMA as Ryuko's, keeping 6; and its AIR form clearing 8 and 5 (PCs 1157–1158). His HISA Down flies at −800 (`LAUNCH_VX`). **KO:** 負け (35) tests global 0 above 9 into make hikari (39, PC 377) and jumps to KO NORMAL (36, PC 378), which tests variable 3 for 30 into >KO NORMAL 2nd (37, PC 382). Both launch straight up at raw 0/−1820, gravity 90 (PCs 400, 413), as RARIO's, and land into KO down (38), which sinks without a slide: image 207 lower every 2 ticks (PCs 423–430), then 8191 for 50 (PC 431). Its op `0x11` would enter Down normal (113) with life left (PC 418), which a KO never has. **Buttons:** his commands use P (D), K (A), Hit Up (B) and Chuck (E); no entry names an air action, and his Jump 2 entries name none, so he has no air attacks or double jump. Dash F (10) runs at raw 900 and Dash B (11) at −900. P-HP (entry 9, 139) takes 100 of the opponent's life at any range (op `0x15`, PC 1629), with no box. K+HP (entry 10, 140) adds 1000 to it (PC 1633): `0x413216` passes the opponent's change through `0x40e7c0` as it does his own (`0x41323a`, `0x413298`), which caps a gain at full life (`0x40ea9f`). CHUCK KO (entry 7, 143) takes 1000 of his own (PC 1662). HITUP attack (entry 8, 142) spawns *OBJ counting (141, PC 1645) in slot 9, which counts 3, 2 and 1 (images 113, 114 and 160, 100 ticks each with sound 1, additive), holds its crouch 298 ticks (PC 1647), then strikes 10 (responses 3/3/4, guards 25/26/4, PCs 1652–1653). 技名3 (entry 6, → Hit Up, 144) counts down the same (PC 1667), holds 295 ticks (PC 1670) and throws 技名4 (145, PC 1675) 127 source pixels ahead: RARIO's fire, rolling at raw 600 under gravity 80 to strike 10 (response 50). Its landing handler enters 技名5 (146), which spawns 技名6 (147) in slot 1, the ball it carries, and hops at −1100, slowing at each display (PCs 1719–1762), until a hit or guard enters Nothing (197). Each holds while global 6 runs (PCs 1694, 1733, 1777, ...). 技名4 to 技名6 name no command. **Idle:** 通常立ち (1) clears variables 8, 3, 1, 2 and 9 (PCs 2–6), and after 98 ticks (PC 24) adds 1000 to his life, takes 1000 of his power and adds 1000 to the opponent's: full life, an empty gauge and three stocks for them. The idle clip carries that op `0x15`, which the standing interpreter passes over and the match runs at the clip's age (`idlePower`), since his idle has no rolls. **Results and intro:** 勝ち (34) calls Demo stand (22) three times (PC 366), plays sound 19 (PC 368) and loops three poses from PC 370 (PC 374); the draw (40) calls 22 three times (PC 446) and loops image 227 from PC 450 (PC 451); each first enters DEMO air (23) while airborne (op `0x16`, PCs 365, 445). 開始時 (24) has no BR to jump to: its op `0x1f` on global 3 has `r[5]` 0 (PC 220), and `0x4137a4` sets only for `r[5]` & 3 and tests only for bits 2–3. It tests global 10 for 10 into 開始時 ERROR (26, PC 221) and variable 2 for 1 into 開始時 2P (25, PC 223), which Vs. play leaves at 0; it copies `[0x470044]`, which each round setup counts up (`0x408986`), into its object variable 1 and enters its VS banners (VS 1P, 31: a 練習用 sign and a 1P panel, PCs 227–228) at 1 (PC 224) or for global 3 above 99 (PC 225), and otherwise jumps past them (PC 226). It then dashes back at raw −10000 for 10 ticks and in at 2870 for 15 on blank displays (PCs 229–232), spawns VS CHANGE (30, PC 234), an unseen object that loops on global 15, stands 70 ticks (PC 235), clears global 13 (PC 239) and calls Demo stand four times (PC 240): 320 ticks, with no stock charge. `ponytail:` the port plays it past the banners and in place, without its op `0x01` runs, which would leave him 430 source pixels from the left bound wherever the authored start puts him. HUD face 2001 (44, PC 472); he has no *NAME, and VS NAME (33) shows 235, a blank image (PC 356), so the HUD shows no name. He has no Black, and nothing of his sets variable 14. His CPU table walks, jumps, dashes and presses P and K by distance (entries 4, 9, 10), draining and refilling the opponent's life. Authored keys: 技名3 on U. The last two players, `49 CPU-ZENKA` and `50 CPU-ALPHA`, are blank templates like 43–45: 24 actions of one empty record each, no command entries, and the nine placeholder images of `44 B4`.
- **Title:** the title object (`0x4080a0`) by its state (`+0x152`). State 0 loads T1 (the demo the KGT's byte at `0x4438a1` names: 1, in the system block's list of 100 at `0x43d49c`). `0x406790` makes its objects as CHAR SELECT's and keeps the flags (`0x427c96`) and ticks (`0x427c99`) of the 0x409 bytes the loader reads after the common container (`0x4040d2`): T1's 0 and 700, T2's 1 and 0. State 1 counts ten ticks (`+0x156`), then lets a fresh button skip a demo with flag bit 0, armed by a tick without one (`+0x15a`), and otherwise runs its ticks out (`0x424f08`): T1, which isn't skippable, shows the volume warning (image 2, 300 ticks, sound 1), ALICUU (0, 150, sound 2) and NEO TEAM F.K. (1, sound 3) for 710 ticks in all. State 2 loads T2 (byte `0x44389c`: 2), whose objects clear the globals (PCs 20–34) and show the four backdrop quadrants, the logo and, once global 0 is 1, the menu (PC 57, image 5 at (244, 383)); its backdrop cues sound 1, the 126-second track. With `0x4438a4`'s bit 0x40 state 3 waits for any fresh press (`0x4280d8`, all players' presses), then state 4 lists the modes bits 4, 8 and 0x10 allow (story 0, VS single 1, VS multi 2: all three) and spawns the KGT title cursor (`0x445244`: 78, which sets global 0, cues sound 10 and pulses an additive image 137). State 5 moves the cursor (the static `0x424780`, zero at load) up on up or left (5) and down on down or right (0xa), wrapping, once per press until every key is let go (`+8`, cleared while `0x4cfa04`, any key held, is 0), stands it where its mode's KGT action's first record says (79, 80, 88: (249, 387), (249, 405), (249, 423)), and on a button sets the mode (`0x470058`) and spawns the select screen (object type 10). With T2's 0 ticks no countdown (`+0x15e`) returns it to T1. `export_demo` exports T1 and T2 as CHAR SELECT, with image 0, which shows like any other (`0x40ccc4` skips only a missing image), so CHAR SELECT's P1 sign now opens on its first frame too. Authored: only VS single is ported, so a button on story or VS multi chooses nothing; T2's track, like CHAR SELECT's, plays only with Music ticked; R starts the title over, and Character select skips it.
- **Character select:** the original's VS single select screen (object type 10, `0x406fc0`, mode 1 of `0x470058`), from `select.ts`, which runs its objects' raw records as the idle interpreter does. The loader copies the KGT's tail (after its images and actions) to `0x435474`, so the system block the screen reads maps straight onto the file (`export_ui` checks that the round letterings' words 13, 14, 15 sit at `0x4451c2`): the 50 players' names, 256 bytes each; the cursors' KGT actions (`0x445250`: 84 and 85, and 86 and 87 once decided); the grid's origin (233, 50), spacing (26, 70), 8 columns and 6 rows (`0x4452b0`–`0x4452ba`); where each side's hovered player stands, (100, 470) and (540, 470) (`0x4452bc`, `0x4452c4`); and a flag byte a player (`0x4452cc` + index), bit 0 for story mode and bit 1 for VS. A cell's index is its player's (columns × row + column): SHIN-Z's flag has only bit 0, DoraEmon's, the blanks' and the CPU files' none, and TRAINING's only bit 1. State 0 (`0x40701f`) loads demo 10 (the byte at `0x44389e`), CHAR SELECT, whose named actions holding a display `0x406790` makes into objects as a stage's (`export_select`), and spawns both cursors at priority `0x65`. Its backdrop clears the select globals, setting 3 and 10 to 10 (PCs 2–12); the SELECT BOX shows its grid of faces (13), or, once global 3 passes 40, the grid with the VS locks drawn in (30), which the 2P cursor's script brings about by setting global 3 to 50 and global 10 to 0 (KGT PCs 541–542). State 1 (`0x407782`) places each cursor at origin + spacing × cell, moves it a cell per direction (`0x406e70`), wrapping, and, when its index changes, first deletes the side's player objects (`0x406520`) for a tick, then, on a named player with bit 1, spawns its select face (the default-action table's word 21, `+0x75a4`) at the side's spot, the 2P one mirrored (`0x4079ad`). Each select face tests global 3 for 100 into its TEAM FACE, spawns its *NAME (Ryuko's 66 at (−100, −85)), which slides in at raw 470, 250 and 130 as it fades up, and shakes in before holding. Any button (`0x3f0`) on a hovered player chooses: the cursor gives way to its decided form, which flashes, plays sound 9 and adds 5 to global 7 (PCs 569, 605); at 10 the demo's scripts 2 and 3 slide two panels shut (PCs 71–83). Once both have chosen, the 102nd tick starts the battle (`0x407a94`, object type 14), and a won VS match spawns the screen again (`0x40982b`). Input (`0x414770`): buttons count as fresh presses, and a direction counts at once, then repeats after 50 ticks (`0x41e3fc`) and every 5 (`0x41e400`), without retriggering an axis held across a change. The cursors start on Ryuko's cell and keep their places between visits, as the statics at `0x424e50` and `0x424e58` do. Demo and KGT objects draw their display's top left at their offset; a select face and its name stand on their ground point. Authored: one player moves the 1P cursor, then the 2P one, which takes over the keys as held; the screen's 57-second track (sound 1) plays only with Music ticked; the fighter and opponent lists stay below the arena, so DoraEmon and SHIN-Z remain playable. The button that chooses (`0x406ee0`: A–F as 0–5; the port's K, H, J and O press A, B, D and E) picks the palette of the eight in each player's container (`+0x11c`, `0x420` bytes apart) that its kind 0 and 1 objects draw with (`+0xe00b`, read at `0x40ccf2`): the select face recolours once chosen, moved on to the next if the other side has its palette (`0x406f20`). The battle sets each player's to its button (`0x408a9f`), and each fighter, player 1's first, moves off the other's as it enters (`0x412056`), so the same button twice turns player 1's. Images with their own palette (flag 1) keep it, and kind 5, the HUD face, draws in palette 0. `export_palettes` gives each colour palette 0 shows its colour in all eight (no palette-0 colour stands for two), and the scene recolours each image once, as it tints; black draws nothing. The globals the screen leaves (3 at 50, 10 at 0) pick each fighter's VS paths (below), which a Vs. CPU battle the screen starts plays. Authored: free practice, and a pair from the dropdowns, keep the 開始時 BR path and the moves' global 3 = 0 forms (global 3 = 0, which the story's NEXT demos and the title's restore).
- **VS paths:** `Match`'s `vs` option sets global 3 to 50 for op `0x1f` (selector `0x83`), as the select screen leaves it; the scene passes it to a Vs. CPU match whose pair the select screen chose. DoraEmon, whom VS single never offers (his select flag is 0), exports no 開始時: his jumps to Start KO (PC 338), whose 38 + 18 spawns itself each tick until the objects fill the engine's 1023 slots (`0x406570`), so a VS pairing with him plays the 開始時 BR intro. With round calls, VS single places the players at 390 and 890 source pixels (`0x408ac3`), and each set-up enters the default-action table's word 17 (`+0x759c`, `0x412180`), 開始時, which runs as a script from the round's first step while commands, the CPU and the timer wait. Ryuko's (30) shows nothing for 5 ticks; global 3 isn't 0 (PC 286) and global 10, which the select screen zeroes (KGT PC 542), isn't 10 (PC 287). Player variable 2 is cleared at set-up (`0x4121cc`) and nothing sets it, so both players take the 1P path (PC 289), player 2's facing left. Op `0x1f` with r[5] bit `0x80` takes its operand from r[6] (`0x413813`–`0x4138cf`): `0xc7` is the round (`0x470044`, `0x4138c5`), which PC 290 copies into the object's variable 1 and tests for 1. In round 1 it jumps to PC 293, which spawns VS 1P (54) and its other half (54 + 9) in viewport coordinates at (0, 270): the portrait (images 2003, 2004) slides in at raw 2000 for 10 ticks, drifts at 5 for 100 while VS NAME (56, image 464) fades in and out, then flies off at 3000 plus 100 a tick. Player 2's face left and leave the world (`0x4124c9`). Then both fighters dash back at −10000 for 10 ticks, which the camera window (`0x40fba2`) stops at 370 and 910, and in at 2870 for 15, so they cross to about 800 and 480 and stand facing away. Black (43) spawns, image 3 holds 70 ticks, and OP super Charge (39) jumps straight to VS CHARGE (38) for global 3 > 40 (PC 417): no power and no stocks. VS CHARGE loops on a blank display with a body box 9900 source pixels below it, skipped while player variable 3 is 30 or 50. In round 1, PC 305 enters Round 1 (33) from its start: 50 blank ticks, a step back at −2000 for 5 and the walk in at 200 for 50, with its EX check at PC 348; the intro runs out into idle 354 ticks in. From round 2 it calls Demo (28) twice, its op `0x16` checking down between (PC 307) for 'Chage Character' + 3 (37), which sets variable 0 to 10 and calls the second; it runs out 350 ticks in. Once the round starts the free state turns them. KO Normal sets variable 3 to 100 off global 3 = 0 (PC 598), which only VS CHARGE reads. Ryuko's four finishers test global 3 > 0 for ZA forms that strike with an op `0x18` box where the others take life with op `0x15`, its response queued after the op `0x14` one: Chuck Hit ZA (198, PC 2761) for 20 where Chuck Hit takes 30; DST Hit ZA (216, PC 3109, which Evil Dst hit reaches through PC 3233) for 10, after which both missiles meet the victim where one does; MUSASHI HIT ZA (258, PC 3875) for Musashi Hit's 120; and GCA hit 2 ZA (322, PC 4774) for 39, a kind-2 box set 77 ticks in that strikes once, as the op `0x14` two ticks on clears it (`0x40e550`), then 1 by op `0x15`. Zoma's 開始時 (28) is the same template (PCs 265–286: VS 1P 53, whose VS NAME 55 shows image 340; Black 40; OP super Charge 36 to VS CHARGE 35, PC 378), holding image 113 for the 70 ticks. In round 1 PC 284 enters Start N (31) from its start, 250 ticks of displays that don't move him, and the intro runs out into idle 350 ticks in. From round 2 it shows image 114 for 30 ticks (PC 285) and calls DEMO STAND (41) four times (PC 286), running out 350 ticks in. He has no EX check and no ZA forms, and his KO NORMAL doesn't read global 3. Kiki's (30) is the same, with VS 1P 53, Black 42, OP super Charge 38 and VS CHARGE 37 (PC 386), holding image 113. In round 1 PC 302 enters Round 1 (33) from its start, 250 ticks of displays with Start Smoke (331) at PC 333, running out into idle 350 ticks in. From round 2 it calls DEMO Stand (28) twice (PC 303), running out 350 ticks in. Her CHUCK HIT (208) tests global 3 > 0 (PC 2861) for CHUCK HIT ZA (209), which applies response 76 (PC 2878) and strikes with a kind-2 box for 20 answered by 32, where CHUCK HIT applies 32 and takes 20 by op `0x15`. Chung's (30) is the same, with VS 1P 54, Black 43, OP super Charge 39 and VS CHARGE 38 (PC 395). In round 1 PC 306 enters ROUND 1 (33): 200 ticks of displays with OP eff (36) twice (PCs 335–336), then its op `0x16` checks down (PC 338) for Change EX (35), which sets variable 0 to 100 and joins its call of Demo stand (27); it runs out 350 ticks in. From round 2 it calls Demo stand twice, checks down (PC 308) for Change EX + 3, and calls it three times, running out 350 ticks in. Demo stand tests variable 0 for 100 (PC 259) to play Demo stand EX (28) inside the call: a call's action end re-enters the action saved at `+0x7d` until its count runs out, then returns (`0x412617`, `0x4126ae`). Set-up clears all 16 player variables (`0x4121cc`), so the export takes that jump only on Change EX's paths, which set the variable on their way. His seven finishers test global 3 > 0 for ZA forms that strike with an op `0x18` box for what the others take by op `0x15`: P3 Hit ZA (187, PC 2431), whose kind-2 box spans two displays but strikes once, for P3 Hit's 35: its op `0x14` releases Ryuko at the first after his step (held behind Chung), so the hit test still finds him held; Chuck hit ZA (223) and EX chuck Hit ZA (227) for 40; Orochi hit ZA (252, a kind-3 box) for 40; lee lend ZA (286) for 99; Evil LEE last lend ZA (303) for 60; and en300 end ZA (334), two kind-6 boxes for 50 and 120. Amiro's (29) is the same, with VS 1P 56, Black 45, OP super Charge 41 and VS CHARGE 40 (PC 491). In round 1 PC 326 enters Round 1 (32): Ami (34), 115 blank ticks, then its op `0x16` checks down (PC 356) for EX CHANGE (39) + 3, which sets variable 0 to 100 and rejoins the displays at PC 357; Demo stand (27) once ends it 350 ticks in. From round 2 it shows image 113 one tick more (PC 327), calls Demo stand twice, checks down (PC 329) for EX CHANGE, which rejoins at PC 330, and calls it once more, running out 350 ticks in. His five finishers test global 3 > 0 for ZA forms whose boxes strike for what the others take by op `0x15`: EX P3 Hit ZA (188) for 40, applying response 59 where the others apply 76; Chuck hit ZA (211) for 20; cc hit ZA (244) for 30; ERX Fin Grab Hit ZA (361) for 70; and WHIP Fin ZA (373) for 40. In round 1 PC 378 enters Round 1 (34): image 151 for 50 ticks, a roll (PC 402) that picks sound 3 or 4, then images 151 to 145, running out 423 ticks in, past FIGHT. From round 2 the round copy's jump (PC 226) takes + 11, which he exports on its own as a jump target, and calls his stand (22) from + 4 (PC 240), running out 320 ticks in. He has no ZA forms.
